    GAE-sb  = the update's own advantage split exactly, A = sum_k GAE(r_k, V=0) + critic bootstraps - V(s), each part

    GAE-sb  = the update's own advantage split exactly, A = sum_k GAE(r_k, V=0) + critic bootstraps - V(s), each part

              actual pull in expectation).  CRITIC_BOOTSTRAP = the critic's bootstrapped values (beyond the
              gamma*lambda horizon), not attributable to a reward term.  PRIMARY.

    GAE-sb  = the update's own advantage split exactly, A = sum_k GAE(r_k, V=0) + critic bootstraps - V(s), each part
              minus a cross-fitted state-only baseline (same expected pull, lower variance; the parts sum to the
              actual pull in expectation).  CRITIC_BOOTSTRAP = the critic's bootstrapped values (beyond the
              gamma*lambda horizon), not attributable to a reward term.  PRIMARY.

              policy gradient split by term, with no critic (rows >= 200 steps from a cut).  CHECK.
